SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_staff FROM Staff;
SELECT COUNT(*) AS count_staff FROM Staff;
SELECT A.agency_id, COUNT(S.staff_id) AS num_staff FROM Agencies A JOIN Staff S ON A.agency_id = S.agency_id GROUP BY A.agency_id;
SELECT A.agency_id, COUNT(S.staff_id) AS num_staff FROM Agencies A JOIN Staff S ON A.agency_id = S.agency_id GROUP BY A.agency_id;
SELECT A.agency_id, A.agency_details FROM Agencies A JOIN Staff S ON A.agency_id = S.agency_id WHERE S.staff_id IN ( SELECT staff_id FROM Staff_in_Meetings GROUP BY staff_id ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC LIMIT 1 );
SELECT A.agency_id, A.agency_details FROM Agencies A JOIN Staff S ON A.agency_id = S.agency_id WHERE S.staff_id IN ( SELECT staff_id FROM Staff_in_Meetings ) GROUP BY A.agency_id, A.agency_details HAVING COUNT(S.staff_id) = ( SELECT MAX(count_staff) FROM ( SELECT COUNT(*) AS count_staff FROM Staff_in_Meetings SM GROUP BY SM.staff_id ) ) ORDER BY COUNT(S.staff_id) DESC LIMIT 1;
SELECT m.meeting_outcome, COUNT(sm.staff_id) AS num_staff FROM Meetings m JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sm ON m.meeting_id = sm.meeting_id GROUP BY m.meeting_outcome;
SELECT m.meeting_id, m.meeting_outcome, COUNT(*) AS num_meetings FROM Meetings m JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sm ON m.meeting_id = sm.meeting_id GROUP BY m.meeting_id, m.meeting_outcome;
SELECT C.client_id, COUNT(M.meeting_id) AS num_meetings FROM Clients C JOIN Meetings M ON C.client_id = M.client_id GROUP BY C.client_id;
SELECT C.client_id, COUNT(M.meeting_id) AS meeting_count FROM Clients C JOIN Meetings M ON C.client_id = M.client_id GROUP BY C.client_id;
SELECT A.agency_details AS agency_name, COUNT(M.meeting_id) AS total_meetings FROM Agencies A JOIN Clients C ON A.agency_id = C.agency_id JOIN Meetings M ON C.client_id = M.client_id GROUP BY A.agency_details;
SELECT meeting_type, COUNT(*) AS num_meetings FROM Meetings GROUP BY meeting_type;
SELECT m.meeting_id, m.meeting_outcome, m.meeting_type, c.client_details FROM Meetings m JOIN Clients c ON m.client_id = c.client_id JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sim ON m.meeting_id = sim.meeting_id;
SELECT m.meeting_id, m.meeting_outcome, m.meeting_type, c.client_details FROM Meetings m JOIN Clients c ON m.client_id = c.client_id JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sm ON m.meeting_id = sm.meeting_id;
SELECT m.meeting_id, COUNT(sm.staff_id) AS num_staff FROM Meetings m JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sm ON m.meeting_id = sm.meeting_id GROUP BY m.meeting_id;
SELECT m.meeting_id, COUNT(sim.staff_id) AS num_staff FROM Meetings m JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sim ON m.meeting_id = sim.meeting_id GROUP BY m.meeting_id;
SELECT s.staff_id, COUNT(*) AS num_attended_meetings FROM Staff s JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sim ON s.staff_id = sim.staff_id JOIN Meetings m ON sim.meeting_id = m.meeting_id WHERE m.meeting_id IN ( SELECT mm.meeting_id FROM Meetings mm WHERE mm.meeting_id IN ( SELECT sim.meeting_id FROM Staff_in_Meetings sim GROUP BY sim.meeting_id HAVING COUNT(sim.staff_id) > 0 AND MIN(sim.meeting_id) < MAX(sim.meeting_id) ) ) GROUP BY s.staff_id;
SELECT s.staff_id FROM Staff s JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sim ON s.staff_id = sim.staff_id WHERE sim.meeting_id IN ( SELECT mm.meeting_id FROM Meetings mm WHERE mm.billable_yn = 'Y' GROUP BY mm.meeting_id HAVING COUNT(*) < ( SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Meetings m WHERE m.billable_yn = 'Y' AND m.start_date_time <= mm.end_date_time AND m.end_date_time >= mm.start_date_time ) );
SELECT COUNT(*) AS num_staff_attended_meeting FROM Staff s JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sm ON s.staff_id = sm.staff_id;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT s.staff_id) AS num_distinct_staff FROM Staff s JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sim ON s.staff_id = sim.staff_id;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT staff_id) AS no_attendance_count FROM Staff_in_Meetings WHERE meeting_id IN ( SELECT meeting_id FROM Staff_in_Meetings GROUP BY meeting_id HAVING COUNT(*) = 0 );
SELECT COUNT(*) AS no_meeting_attendance FROM Staff EXCEPT SELECT s.staff_id FROM Staff s JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sm ON s.staff_id = sm.staff_id;
SELECT C.client_id, C.client_details FROM Clients C WHERE EXISTS ( SELECT * FROM Meetings M WHERE M.client_id = C.client_id ) OR EXISTS ( SELECT * FROM Invoices I WHERE I.client_id = C.client_id );
SELECT c.client_id, c.client_details FROM Clients c JOIN Meetings m ON c.client_id = m.client_id WHERE m.meeting_outcome IN ('Report', 'Summary') OR EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM Invoices i WHERE i.client_id = c.client_id AND i.invoice_status IN ('Working', 'Starting') );
SELECT s.staff_id, s.staff_details FROM Staff s JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sim ON s.staff_id = sim.staff_id WHERE EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM Meetings m WHERE m.meeting_id IN ( SELECT meeting_id FROM Staff_in_Meetings smm JOIN Staff st ON smm.staff_id = st.staff_id WHERE st.staff_details LIKE '%s%' ) ) AND s.staff_id IN ( SELECT staff_id FROM Staff_in_Meetings GROUP BY staff_id HAVING COUNT(*) >= 1 );
SELECT S.staff_id, S.staff_details FROM Staff S JOIN Staff_in_Meetings SIMM ON S.staff_id = SIMM.staff_id WHERE SIMM.meeting_id IN ( SELECT meeting_id FROM Staff_in_Meetings WHERE staff_id IN ( SELECT staff_id FROM Staff WHERE staff_details LIKE '%s%' ) ) AND S.staff_id IN ( SELECT staff_id FROM Staff WHERE staff_details LIKE '%s%' );
SELECT C.client_id, C.sic_code, A.agency_id FROM Agencies A JOIN Clients C ON A.agency_id = C.agency_id WHERE EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM Meetings M JOIN Staff_in_Meetings SM ON M.meeting_id = SM.meeting_id WHERE M.client_id = C.client_id AND M.billable_yn = '1' ) AND EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM Invoices I WHERE I.client_id = C.client_id );
SELECT c.client_id, c.sic_code, c.agency_id FROM Clients c JOIN Meetings m ON c.client_id = m.client_id WHERE m.billable_yn = '1' AND m.meeting_outcome IN ('Report', 'Summary') AND m.start_date_time BETWEEN '2018-03-06 05:07:33' AND '2018-03-21 09:26:41' AND EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM Payments p JOIN Invoices i ON p.invoice_id = i.invoice_id WHERE i.client_id = c.client_id AND p.payment_details IS NOT NULL );
SELECT m.start_date_time AS 'Start Time', m.end_date_time AS 'End Time', c.client_details AS 'Client Detail', s.staff_details AS 'Staff Detail' FROM Meetings m JOIN Clients c ON m.client_id = c.client_id JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sim ON m.meeting_id = sim.meeting_id JOIN Staff s ON sim.staff_id = s.staff_id;
SELECT M.meeting_id, M.start_date_time AS meeting_start, M.end_date_time AS meeting_end, C.client_details, S.staff_details FROM Meetings M JOIN Clients C ON M.client_id = C.client_id JOIN Staff_in_Meetings SIM ON M.meeting_id = SIM.meeting_id JOIN Staff S ON SIM.staff_id = S.staff_id;
